# Experiments Archive Index

Date: 2026-06-26

Superseded iteration notes moved from `experiments/` during repo cleanup Task 2.
JSON/JSONL machine-readable artifacts remain in `experiments/` for reproduction.
Frozen holdout evidence, reliability scorecards, and current ExECTv2/Gan
comparators stay in the active surface (see `experiments/README.md`).
